## Customs Act {#customs-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. 1 (2nd
Supp.)
:::

An Act respecting Customs

::: ReaderNote
\[1986, c. 1, assented to 13th February, 1986\]
:::
:::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-139182 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-139185 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Act,

  [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canada Border Services
      Agency; ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bonded warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bonded warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place licensed as
      a bonded warehouse by the Minister under subsection 91(1) of the
      [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([entrepôt de
      stockage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1996, c. 31, s.
      73\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Canada-United States Free Trade Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canada-United States Free Trade
      Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c. 14, s.
      35\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Cannabis
      Act](/eng/acts/C-24.5); ([cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cannabis product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([produit du cannabis]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[carrier code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[carrier code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the unique
      identification number issued by the Minister either under
      subsection 12.1(4) or before the coming into force of that
      subsection; ([code de transporteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
      [Canada-Chile Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-1.6); ([ALÉCC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CCOFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CCOFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Colombia Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-1.65); ([ALÉCCO]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
      Canada --- Costa Rica Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act; ([ALÉCCR]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CEFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CEFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
      [Canada--EFTA Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-3.65); ([ALÉCA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Certificate of Origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Certificate of Origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the proof of
      origin form for goods for which preferential tariff treatment
      under a free trade agreement is claimed, prescribed under
      subsection 35.1(1) and subject to the regulations made pursuant to
      paragraph 35.1(4)(b); ([certificat d'origine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CETA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CETA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--European Union Comprehensive Economic and Trade Agreement
      Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-4.8); ([AÉCG]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CHFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CHFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Honduras Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.10); ([ALÉCH]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Chile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Chile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Chili]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CIFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CIFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 2(1) of the
      [Canada-Israel Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.4); ([ALÉCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cigar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cigar]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2002, c. 22, s.
      328\]]{.Repealed}

  [[CJFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CJFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Jordan Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.45); ([ALÉCJ]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CKFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CKFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Korea Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-6.48); ([ALÉCRC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Colombia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Colombia]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Colombie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2005, c. 38, s.
      60\]]{.Repealed}

  [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[conveyance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any vehicle, aircraft or
      water-borne craft or any other contrivance that is used to move
      persons or goods; ([moyen de transport]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Costa Rica]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Costa Rica]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Costa Rica]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[courier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[courier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      regulation; ([messager]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CPAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Panama Economic Growth and Prosperity
      Act](/eng/acts/C-7.95); ([ALÉCPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CPFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Peru Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-8.4); ([ALÉCP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CPTPP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPTPP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Comprehensive and Progressive Agreement for Trans-Pacific
      Partnership Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-36.45); ([PTPGP]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CPTPP country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CPTPP country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([pays PTPGP]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[CUFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement Implementation Act,
      2023](/eng/acts/C-10.541); ([ALÉCU]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUFTA 2017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUFTA 2017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.54), as it reads immediately before the day on
      which section 39 of the Canada--Ukraine Free Trade Agreement
      Implementation, 2023 comes into force; ([ALÉCU de
      2017]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUKTCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUKTCA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United Kingdom Trade Continuity Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.56); ([ACCCRU]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUKTCA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUKTCA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([bénéficiaire de
      l'ACCCRU]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUSMA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by the
      definition [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
      [Canada--United States--Mexico Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10.65); ([ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CUSMA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country that is a
      party to CUSMA; ([pays ACEUM]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[customs office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[customs office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place designated as
      a customs office by the Minister under section 5; ([bureau de
      douane]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[data]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means representations, in any form,
      of information or concepts; ([données]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1999, c.
      17, s. 123\]]{.Repealed}

  [[designated goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2002, c. 22, s. 328\]]{.Repealed}

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} aviation fuel,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} aviation gasoline,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} beer or malt liquor,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} diamonds,

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} diesel fuel,

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} gasoline,

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} pearls,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} precious and semi-precious stones,

      - [(i.1)]{.lawlabel} spirits,

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} wine, or

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} such other goods as the Minister may, by order,
        designate for the purposes of all or any of the provisions of
        this Act; ([marchandises désignées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any duties or taxes levied or
      imposed on imported goods under the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
      [Special Import Measures Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or any other Act of
      Parliament, but, for the purposes of subsection 3(1), paragraphs
      59(3)(b) and 65(1)(b), sections 69 and 73 and subsections 74(1),
      75(2) and 76(1), does not include taxes imposed under Part IX of
      the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15); ([droits]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[duty free shop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place licensed as a
      duty free shop by the Minister under section 24; ([boutique hors
      taxes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EFTA state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EFTA state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Canada--EFTA Free Trade Agreement
      Implementation Act](/eng/acts/C-3.65); ([État de
      l'AELÉ]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EU country or other CETA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EU country or other CETA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([pays de l'Union européenne ou autre
      bénéficiaire de l'AÉCG]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[excise stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([timbre
      d'accise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means export from
      Canada; ([exporter]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[forfeit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[forfeit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means forfeit to Her Majesty in
      right of Canada; ([confisquer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[free trade agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[free trade agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an agreement
      set out in column 2 of Part 1 of the schedule; ([accord de
      libre-échange]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[free trade partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[free trade partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country or
      territory set out in column 1 of Part 1 of the
      schedule; ([partenaire de libre-échange]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, for greater certainty, includes
      conveyances, animals and any document in any
      form; ([marchandises]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Honduras]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Honduras]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Honduras]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Iceland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Iceland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Islande]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[immediate container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([contenant immédiat]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means import into
      Canada; ([importer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[imported from Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[imported from Israel or another CIFTA
      beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([importé d'Israël ou d'un autre
      bénéficiaire de l'ALÉCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inland waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inland waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means all the rivers, lakes
      and other fresh waters in Canada and includes the St. Lawrence
      River as far seaward as the straight lines drawn

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from Cap-des-Rosiers to the westernmost point
        of Anticosti Island, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} from Anticosti Island to the north shore of the
        St. Lawrence River along the meridian of longitude sixty-three
        degrees west; ([eaux internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[internal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[internal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1996, c.
      31, s. 73\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Israel or another CIFTA beneficiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
      the same meaning as in subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Israël ou autre bénéficiaire de
      l'ALÉCI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Jordan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Jordan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Jordanie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Korea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Korea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Corée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licensed user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([utilisateur agréé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Liechtenstein]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Liechtenstein]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Liechtenstein]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[mail]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mail]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning set out in section 2
      of the [Canada Post Corporation
      Act](/eng/acts/C-10); ([envois]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [courrier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[manufactured tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2002, c.
      22, s. 328\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in Part V.1, the
      Minister of Public Safety and Emergency
      Preparedness; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned to the word
      [[Agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} by subsection 2(1) of the
      [North American Free Trade Agreement Implementation
      Act](/eng/acts/N-23.8); ([ALÉNA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NAFTA country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned to
      that expression by subsection 2(1) of the [North American Free
      Trade Agreement Implementation Act](/eng/acts/N-23.8); ([pays
      ALÉNA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Norway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Norway]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Norvège]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person employed in the
      administration or enforcement of this Act, the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or the [Special Import Measures
      Act](/eng/acts/S-15) and includes any member of the Royal Canadian
      Mounted Police; ([agent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [agent des
      douanes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Panama]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Panama]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Panama]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a partnership,
      a corporation, a trust, the estate of a deceased individual or a
      body that is a society, a union, a club, an association, a
      commission or other organization of any
      kind; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Peru]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Peru]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in subsection
      2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Pérou]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[preferential tariff treatment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in
      respect of goods, entitlement to whichever tariff set out in
      column 3 of Part 1 of the schedule is applicable in the
      circumstances; ([traitement tarifaire
      préférentiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CCFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CCOFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CCOFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CCRFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CEFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CEFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CIFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CIFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under CPFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      CPFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[preferential tariff treatment under NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[preferential tariff treatment under
      NAFTA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s.
      24\]]{.Repealed}

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a form or the manner of filing a
        form, authorized by the Minister,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the information to be provided on
        or with a form, specified by the Minister, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
        determined in accordance with rules prescribed by regulation;

      and for the purposes of paragraphs (a) and (b), form is not
      limited to a single record or document with blank spaces to be
      filled out; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the President of the
      Canada Border Services Agency appointed under subsection 7(1) of
      the [Canada Border Services Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-1.4); ([président]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[raw leaf tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[raw leaf tobacco]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([tabac
      en feuilles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material on which data
      are recorded or marked and which is capable of being read or
      understood by a person or a computer system or other
      device; ([document]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a regulation made by the
      Governor in Council under this Act; ([règlement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods, to authorize the removal
        of the goods from a customs office, sufferance warehouse, bonded
        warehouse or duty free shop for use in Canada, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of goods to which paragraph 32(2)(b)
        applies, to receive the goods at the place of business of the
        importer, owner or consignee; ([dédouanement]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[restricted formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[restricted formulation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([préparation assujettie à des
      restrictions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specially denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specially denatured alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([alcool spécialement
      dénaturé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rate of interest,
      expressed as a percentage per year, equal to 6% per year plus the
      prescribed rate; ([taux déterminé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
      2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([spiritueux]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[spirits licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spirits licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([titulaire de licence de
      spiritueux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sufferance warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sufferance warehouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place
      licensed as a sufferance warehouse by the Minister under section
      24; ([entrepôt d'attente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Switzerland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Switzerland]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Suisse]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[tariff classification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tariff classification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      classification of imported goods under a tariff item in the List
      of Tariff Provisions set out in the schedule to the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([classement
      tarifaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[territorial sea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[territorial sea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1996, c.
      31, s. 73\]]{.Repealed}

  [[tobacco licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tobacco licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([titulaire de licence de
      tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tobacco product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([produit
      du tabac]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Ukraine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Ukraine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 2(1) of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([Ukraine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 1997, c. 14, s.
      35\]]{.Repealed}

  [[value for duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[value for duty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of goods,
      the value of the goods as it would be determined in accordance
      with sections 45 to 56; ([valeur en douane]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[vaping device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vaping device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([dispositif de
      vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vaping product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vaping product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([produit
      de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vaping product licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vaping product licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([titulaire de licence de produits de
      vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[vaping substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[vaping substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([substance de vapotage]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2
      of the [Excise Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1); ([vin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wine licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wine licensee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1). ([titulaire
      de licence de vin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of the definition [[designated
  goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1),

  [[alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[ethyl
      alcohol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
      [[spirits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2002, c. 22, s.
      328\]]{.Repealed}

  [[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[malt
      liquor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by section 4
      of the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14); ([bière]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} ou [liqueur de malt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[diamonds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[diamonds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means goods for personal use or
      for adornment of the person and classified under subheading Nos.
      7102.10, 7102.31 and 7102.39 in the List of Tariff Provisions set
      out in the schedule to the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([diamants]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[diesel fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[diesel fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      subsection 2(1) of the [Excise Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/E-15); ([combustible diesel]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning assigned by
      subsection 2(1) of the [Excise Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/E-15); ([essence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pearls]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pearls]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means goods for personal use or for
      adornment of the person and classified under heading No. 71.01 in
      the List of Tariff Provisions set out in the schedule to the
      [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); ([perles]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[precious and semi-precious stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[precious and semi-precious stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      goods for personal use or for adornment of the person and
      classified under heading No. 71.03 in the List of Tariff
      Provisions set out in the schedule to the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011). ([pierres précieuses ou
      fines]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2002, c. 22, s.
      328\]]{.Repealed}

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 18, s. 24\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic records

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} Every person required by this Act to keep records
  who does so electronically shall retain them in an electronically
  readable format for the prescribed retention period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction of Canadian waters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may from time to time by
  regulation temporarily restrict, for the purposes of this Act, the
  extent of Canadian waters, including the inland waters, but no such
  regulation shall be construed as foregoing any Canadian rights in
  respect of waters so restricted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers, duties and functions of
  President

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any power, duty or function of the President under
  this Act may be exercised or performed by any person, or by any
  officer within a class of officers, authorized by the President to do
  so and, if so exercised or performed, is deemed to have been exercised
  or performed by the President.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize an officer or a class of
  officers to exercise powers or perform duties of the Minister,
  including any judicial or quasi-judicial powers or duties of the
  Minister, under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation by Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a person employed by the
  Canada Revenue Agency, or a class of those persons, to exercise powers
  or perform duties of the Minister, including any judicial or
  quasi-judicial powers or duties of the Minister, under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation by Minister of National
  Revenue

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of National Revenue may authorize a
  person employed by the Canada Revenue Agency or the Agency, or a class
  of those persons, to exercise powers or perform duties of that
  Minister, including any judicial or quasi-judicial powers or duties of
  that Minister, under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-139327 .Part}

### [Application to Her Majesty]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139328 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties binding on Her Majesty

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  duties or taxes levied on imported goods under the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15), the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the
  [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Select Luxury Items Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35) or any other law relating to customs are
  binding on Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province in respect of
  any goods imported by or on behalf of Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Act binding on Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), this Act is binding on Her
  Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may by regulation exempt Her
  Majesty in any case or class of cases from the requirement to report
  under section 12 or 95 subject to such conditions as may be
  prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 3
- 2002, c. 22, s. 329
- [2022, c. 10, s. 152](#1356783-1374187){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356783-1374187.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20030701.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Penalty and Interest]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139338 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest to be compounded

**[[3.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.1 .sectionLabel}** Interest shall be
computed at a prescribed rate or at a specified rate and compounded
daily and, if interest is computed in respect of an amount under a
provision of this Act and is unpaid on the day it would, but for this
section, have ceased to be computed under that provision, interest at
the specified rate shall be computed and compounded daily on that unpaid
interest from that day to the day it is paid and shall be paid as that
provision required the amount to be paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 2
- 2001, c. 25, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed rate may be authorized

**[[3.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.2 .sectionLabel}** Where a person is
required under a provision of this Act to pay interest on an amount at
the specified rate, the person shall, where the Minister or any officer
designated by the Minister for the purposes of this section so
authorizes, pay interest on that amount under that provision at the
prescribed rate rather than at the specified rate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 2
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of penalty or interest

- **[[3.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except with respect to the
  collection of any debt due to Her Majesty under Part V.1, the Minister
  or any officer designated by the President for the purposes of this
  section may at any time waive or cancel all or any portion of any
  penalty or interest otherwise payable by a person under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if measures may be
  taken under section 127.1, a request under section 129 is made or the
  time for making a request set out in that section has not expired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on penalty or interest
  refunded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, as a result of a waiver or cancellation under
  subsection (1), a person is given a refund of an amount of penalty or
  interest that was paid by the person, the person shall be given, in
  addition to the refund, interest at the prescribed rate for the period
  beginning on the first day after the day the amount was paid and
  ending on the day the refund is given, calculated on the amount of the
  refund.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 2
- 1995, c. 41, s. 2
- 2001, c. 25, s. 3
- 2005, c. 38, s. 61
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Security]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139356 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional security

- **[[3.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where security has been given to the
  Minister by a person under a provision of this Act and the Minister or
  any officer (in this section referred to as a "designated officer")
  designated by the President for the purposes of this section
  determines that the security that has been given is no longer
  adequate, the Minister or a designated officer may, by notice served
  personally or by registered or certified mail, require additional
  security to be given by or on behalf of the person within such
  reasonable time as may be stipulated in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment where additional security not
  given

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the additional security required to be given by
  or on behalf of a person under subsection (1) is not given within the
  time it is so required to be given, the amount by which

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount owing in respect of which security that
    has been given to the Minister by the person is no longer adequate

  exceeds

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the value of the security that has been given to
    the Minister by the person, as determined by the Minister or a
    designated officer,

  is payable by the person immediately.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 2
- 2005, c. 38, s. 62
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3.4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Payments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139368 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments

**[[3.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.5 .sectionLabel}** Except in the
circumstances that the Minister may specify, every person who makes a
payment under this Act shall make the payment to the account of the
Receiver General in the prescribed manner, within the prescribed time
and at the prescribed place.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 4
- [2022, c. 10, s. 303](#1357503-1492814){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357503-1492814.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3.5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 3.5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Performance of Obligations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139377 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performance of obligations

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** Where more than one person
is responsible for the performance of any obligation under this Act,
performance of the obligation by any one of them shall be deemed to be
performance by all of them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertakings

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** In the case of goods to
which paragraph 32(2)(b) applies, the Minister may accept from an
importer or transporter an undertaking to assume obligations in relation
to compliance with this Act and the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 5
:::

### [Customs Offices and Facilities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139384 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Customs offices

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may designate
customs offices inside or outside Canada for a specified purpose or
generally for business relating to customs and may at any time amend,
cancel or reinstate any such designation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Customs facilities

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner or operator of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any international bridge or tunnel, for the use of
    which a toll or other charge is payable,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any railway operating internationally, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any airport, wharf or dock that receives
    conveyances operating internationally and in respect of which a
    customs office has been designated under section 5

  shall provide, equip and maintain free of charge to Her Majesty at or
  near the bridge, tunnel, railway, airport, wharf or dock adequate
  buildings, accommodation or other facilities for the proper detention
  and examination of imported goods or for the proper search of persons
  by customs officers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1),
  [[maintain]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in addition to performing
  general maintenance, paying all costs related to the operation of the
  buildings, accommodation and other facilities, including electricity,
  lighting, ventilation, heating, cooling, water supply, sewage
  treatment, fire protection, snow removal and cleaning.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1.1) has retroactive effect to the day
  on which subsection (1) came into force and applies in respect of any
  action or judicial proceeding that is pending on the day on which this
  subsection comes into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make such improvements as the Minister considers
    desirable to any facilities provided pursuant to subsection (1),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} post, on or about such facilities, such signs as
    the Minister considers appropriate for the safe use of the
    facilities or for the enforcement of any law relating to the
    importation or exportation of goods or the international movement of
    persons, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} continue to use such facilities for as long a
    period of time as the Minister requires,

  and no person shall interfere with any of the rights set out in this
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, subject to subsection
  (4), make regulations determining what are adequate buildings,
  accommodation and other facilities for the purposes referred to in
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect of regulations

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} A regulation made under subsection (3) may, if it
  so provides, have retroactive effect and apply in respect of any
  pending action or judicial proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any building, accommodation or other facility
  provided for the purposes referred to in subsection (1) that fails to
  meet the applicable requirements of Part II of the [Canada Labour
  Code](/eng/acts/L-2) shall be deemed not to be adequate for those
  purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where any building, accommodation or other facility
  provided pursuant to subsection (1) at or near an international bridge
  or tunnel is not adequate for the purposes referred to in that
  subsection, the Minister may, on thirty days notice to the owner or
  operator of the bridge or tunnel, carry out any construction or
  repairs on the site of the facility in order to render it adequate for
  those purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for costs

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The owner or operator of an international bridge or
  tunnel is liable for all reasonable costs incurred by the Minister
  under subsection (5), which costs may be recovered in accordance with
  sections 143 to 145.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 6, c. 26 (3rd Supp.), s. 1
- 2012, c. 31, s. 265
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application of Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139416 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application inside and outside Canada

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Act and the
regulations, any of the powers, duties or functions established under
this Act or the regulations relating to the importation of goods may be
carried out inside Canada or, where they do not conflict with the laws
of another country, inside that other country and may be carried out
before or after the importation.

### [Provision of Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139419 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to provide accurate
information

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** Any information
provided to an officer in the administration or enforcement of this Act,
the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or the [Special Import Measures
Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or under any other Act of Parliament that
prohibits, controls or regulates the importation or exportation of
goods, shall be true, accurate and complete.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 6
:::

### [Forms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139424 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may include on
any form a declaration, to be signed by the person completing the form,
declaring that the information given by that person on the form is true,
accurate and complete.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 8
- 2001, c. 25, s. 7
:::

### [Electronic Administration and Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139429 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic administration and enforcement

- **[[8.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Act may be administered and enforced using electronic means. Any
  person on whom powers, duties or functions are conferred under this
  Act may exercise any of those powers or perform any of those duties or
  functions using the electronic means made available or specified by
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who has been authorized to exercise any
  power or perform any duty or function conferred on a person referred
  to in subsection (1) under this Act may do so using the electronic
  means that are made available or specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 148
- 2001, c. 25, s. 8
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447508){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447508.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 8.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information

**[[8.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 8.3 to 8.6, providing information includes providing a
signature and serving, filing or otherwise providing a record or
document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447517){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447517.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for electronic version

**[[8.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3 .sectionLabel}** A requirement under
this Act to provide information or security --- in any form or manner or
by any means --- is satisfied by providing the electronic version of the
information or security if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the electronic version is provided by the electronic
  means, including an electronic system, that are made available or
  specified by the Minister, if any; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed requirements with respect to
  electronic communications or electronic means have been met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447523){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447523.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed timing of receipt

**[[8.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4 .sectionLabel}** Any information or
security provided by electronic means, including an electronic system,
in accordance with section 8.1 or 8.3, is deemed to be received

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the regulations provide for a day, on that day;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the regulations provide for a day and time, on
  that day and at that time; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the regulations do not provide for a day or a day
  and a time, on the day and at the time that the information or
  security is sent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447530){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447530.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

**[[8.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
by virtue of section 12 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011),
sections 8.1 to 8.4 apply, with any modifications that the circumstances
require, to the administration and enforcement of that Act and
regulations made under it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447534){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447534.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[8.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of the Minister, make
  regulations in respect of electronic communications and electronic
  means, including electronic systems, or any other technology to be
  used in the administration or enforcement of this Act or the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), including regulations respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision of information or security for any
    purpose under this Act or the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)
    in electronic or other form;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the payment of amounts under this Act or the
    [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) by electronic instructions; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which and the extent to which any
    provision of this Act, the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or
    their regulations applies to the electronic communications or
    electronic means, including electronic systems, and adapting any
    such provision for the purpose of applying it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made for the purpose of section 8.3 may
  establish classes and distinguish among those classes.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 304](#1357505-1447544){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357505-1447544.html"}
:::

### [Brokers and Agents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139456 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of customs broker's licence

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to the regulations, the Minister or any person designated by the
  Minister for the purposes of this section may issue to any person who
  is qualified under the regulations a licence to transact business as a
  customs broker.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, etc., of licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the Minister may amend,
  suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate any licence issued under
  subsection (1), and any person designated by the Minister for the
  purpose of this section may amend, suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate
  any licence issued by himself under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an officer so requests, a customs broker shall
  make available to the officer, within the time specified by the
  officer, any records that the customs broker is required by the
  regulations to keep.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall transact or attempt to transact
  business as a customs broker or hold himself out as a customs broker
  unless the person holds a licence issued under subsection (1) or
  unless he is qualified under the regulations and is duly authorized to
  transact business as a customs broker by a person who holds such a
  licence, but nothing in this subsection shall be so construed as to
  prohibit any person from transacting business on his own behalf under
  this Act, or to prohibit persons administering estates or other duly
  authorized agents from transacting business under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing qualifications as to citizenship,
    residence and knowledge of the laws and procedures relating to
    importations and exportations and any other qualifications that must
    be met by an applicant for a customs broker's licence issued under
    subsection (1), and any such qualifications that must be met by a
    person who transacts business as a customs broker on behalf of a
    person so licensed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the terms and conditions on which such
    licences may be issued, including the security that may be required
    and the fees, if any, to be paid for the licences;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the duration of such licences;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of applying for such
    licences or for renewals thereof;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} providing for the examination of applicants for
    such licences, and of persons who will transact business as customs
    brokers on their behalf, by the Minister or by any other person with
    respect to their knowledge of the laws and procedures relating to
    importations and exportations;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the examination fees to be paid, the
    amount of any deposit that may be taken in respect thereof and the
    conditions under which such fees or deposits may be refunded;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the records to be kept by customs
    brokers and the period of time for which they shall be kept; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner and circumstances in which
    the Minister may suspend or cancel a customs broker's licence issued
    under subsection (1) or any other person may suspend or cancel such
    a licence issued by himself thereunder, and the circumstances in
    which a customs broker's licence shall be surrendered.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1, (2nd Supp.), s. 9
- 2001, c. 25, s. 9
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agents

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, any
  person who is duly authorized to do so may transact business under
  this Act as the agent of another person, but an officer may refuse to
  transact business with any such person unless that person, on the
  request of the officer, produces a written authority, in a form
  approved by the Minister, from the person on whose behalf he is
  acting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is duly authorized to administer the
  estate of another person by reason of death, bankruptcy, insolvency or
  incapacity or for any other reason may transact business under this
  Act on behalf of the estate but an officer may refuse to transact
  business with any such person unless that person satisfies the officer
  that he is duly authorized to administer the estate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations in
  respect of any provision of this Act prescribing the circumstances in
  which a person may transact business under that provision as the agent
  of another person.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Importation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-139485 .Part}

### [Persons]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139486 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of persons on arrival in
Canada

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, every
  person arriving in Canada shall, except in such circumstances and
  subject to such conditions as may be prescribed, enter Canada only at
  a customs office designated for that purpose that is open for business
  and without delay present himself or herself to an officer and answer
  truthfully any questions asked by the officer in the performance of
  his or her duties under this or any other Act of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to any person who has
  presented himself or herself outside Canada at a customs office
  designated for that purpose and has not subsequently stopped at any
  other place prior to his or her arrival in Canada unless an officer
  requires that person to present himself or herself to the officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation of passengers and crew

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, every person in charge of a
  conveyance arriving in Canada shall, except in such circumstances and
  subject to such conditions as may be prescribed, ensure that the
  passengers and crew are forthwith on arrival in Canada transported to
  a customs office referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to any person in charge
  of a conveyance transporting passengers and crew all of whom have
  presented themselves outside Canada at a customs office designated for
  that purpose and have not subsequently stopped at any other place
  prior to their arrival in Canada unless an officer requires that
  person to comply therewith.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry and departure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, subsections (1) and (3)
  do not apply to any of the following persons, unless an officer
  requires them to comply with those subsections:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who enters Canadian waters, including the
    inland waters, or the airspace over Canada on board a conveyance
    directly from outside Canada and then leaves Canada on board the
    conveyance, as long as the person was continuously on board that
    conveyance while in Canada and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a conveyance other than an
      aircraft, the person did not land in Canada and the conveyance did
      not anchor, moor or make contact with another conveyance while in
      Canadian waters, including the inland waters, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aircraft, the conveyance did
      not land while in Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who leaves Canadian waters, including the
    inland waters, or the airspace over Canada on board a conveyance and
    then re-enters Canada on board the conveyance, as long as the person
    was continuously on board that conveyance while outside Canada and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a conveyance other than an
      aircraft, the person did not land outside Canada and the
      conveyance did not anchor, moor or make contact with another
      conveyance while outside Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aircraft, the conveyance did
      not land while outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- alternative manner

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} holds an authorization issued by the Minister under
    subsection 11.1(1) to present himself or herself in a prescribed
    alternative manner and who presents himself or herself in the manner
    authorized for that person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a member of a prescribed class of persons
    authorized by regulations made under subsection 11.1(3) to present
    himself or herself in a prescribed alternative manner and who
    presents himself or herself in the manner authorized for that class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of officer

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding that a person holds an authorization
  under subsection 11.1(1) or is authorized under the regulations made
  under subsection 11.1(3), an officer may require a person to present
  himself or herself in accordance with subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 11
- 1996, c. 31, s. 74
- 2001, c. 25, s. 10
- 2012, c. 19, s. 372
- 2017, c. 11, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20120820.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may authorize

- **[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Minister may issue to any person an authorization to present himself
  or herself in an alternative manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, etc., of authorization

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, subject to the regulations, amend,
  suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate an authorization.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing classes of persons who are, and classes
    of persons who may be, authorized to present themselves in
    alternative manners;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting alternative manners of presentation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the requirements and conditions that are
    to be met before authorizations may be issued;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the terms and conditions of
    authorizations;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the amendment, suspension, renewal,
    cancellation or reinstatement of authorizations; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting fees or the manner of determining fees
    to be paid for authorizations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4) does not
  apply to a fee for an authorization issued under this section if it is
  a reciprocal fee under an international arrangement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 11
- 2010, c. 25, s. 172
- 2012, c. 19, s. 373
- 2017, c. 20, s. 454
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.1-20120820.html "Link to previous version of section 11.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of customs controlled areas

- **[[11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may designate an area
  as a customs controlled area for the purposes of this section and
  sections 11.3 to 11.5 and 99.2 and 99.3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, etc. of designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may amend, cancel or reinstate at any
  time a designation made under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 11
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry prohibited

- **[[11.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  owner or operator of a facility where a customs controlled area is
  located shall grant or allow to be granted access to the customs
  controlled area to any person unless the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has been authorized by the Minister; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed person or a member of a prescribed
    class of persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, etc., of authorization

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may amend, suspend, renew, cancel or
  reinstate an authorization.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 11
- 2009, c. 10, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation and reporting --- when
leaving customs controlled area

- **[[11.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  person who is leaving a customs controlled area shall, if requested to
  do so by an officer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} present himself or herself in the prescribed manner
    to an officer and identify himself or herself;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} report in the prescribed manner any goods that he
    or she has acquired through any means while in the customs
    controlled area;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} present those goods and remove any covering from
    them, unload any conveyance or open any part of it, or open or
    unpack any package or container that an officer wishes to examine;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} answer truthfully any questions asked by an officer
    in the performance of his or her duties under this or any other Act
    of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation and reporting --- within
  customs controlled area

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is in a customs controlled area
  shall, if requested to do so by an officer,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} present himself or herself in the prescribed manner
    to an officer and identify himself or herself; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} answer truthfully any questions asked by an officer
    in the performance of his or her duties under this or any other Act
    of Parliament.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsections (1) and
  (1.1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (1.1) do not apply to persons who
  are required to present themselves under section 11 or report goods
  under section 12.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 11
- 2009, c. 10, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[11.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.5 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the persons or classes of persons who may
  be granted access under paragraph 11.3(1)(b); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner in which a person must present
  himself or herself under paragraphs 11.4(1)(a) and (1.1)(a) and report
  goods under paragraph 11.4(1)(b).

- [(c) to (e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2009, c. 10, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 11
- 2009, c. 10, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11.5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 11.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of mixed-traffic corridor

- **[[11.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Minister considers that it is necessary in the public interest, he
  or she may designate as a mixed-traffic corridor a portion of a
  roadway or other access way that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} leads from an international border to a customs
    office designated under section 5; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is used by persons arriving in Canada and by
    persons travelling within Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment, etc., of designation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may amend, cancel or reinstate at any
  time a designation made under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 481
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person travelling in mixed-traffic
corridor

**[[11.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.7 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
travelling in a mixed-traffic corridor shall present themselves to an
officer at the nearest customs office and state whether they are
arriving from a location outside or within Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 481
:::

### [Report of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139580 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, all goods
  that are imported shall, except in such circumstances and subject to
  such conditions as may be prescribed, be reported at the nearest
  customs office designated for that purpose that is open for business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time and manner of report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods shall be reported under subsection (1) at such
  time and in such manner as the Governor in Council may prescribe.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Who reports

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Goods shall be reported under subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods in the actual possession of a
    person arriving in Canada, or that form part of the person's baggage
    where the person and the person's baggage are being carried on board
    the same conveyance, by that person or, in prescribed circumstances,
    by the person in charge of the conveyance;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods imported by courier or as
    mail, by the person who exported the goods to Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods, other than goods referred to
    in paragraph (a) or goods imported as mail, on board a conveyance
    arriving in Canada, by the person in charge of the conveyance; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, by the person on behalf of whom
    the goods are imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods returned to Canada

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, for the purposes of the
  reporting of goods under subsection (1), the return of goods to Canada
  after they are taken out of Canada is an importation of those goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where goods are reported outside Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of goods
  that are reported in the manner prescribed under subsection (2) prior
  to importation at a customs office outside Canada unless an officer
  requires that the goods be reported again under subsection (1) after
  importation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- entry and departure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, unless an officer
  otherwise requires, this section does not apply in respect of goods on
  board a conveyance

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that enters Canadian waters, including the inland
    waters, or the airspace over Canada directly from outside Canada and
    then leaves Canada, as long as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a conveyance other than an
      aircraft, the conveyance did not anchor, moor or make contact with
      another conveyance while in Canadian waters, including the inland
      waters, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aircraft, the conveyance did
      not land while in Canada; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that leaves Canadian waters, including the inland
    waters, or the airspace over Canada and then re-enters Canada, as
    long as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a conveyance other than an
      aircraft, the conveyance did not anchor, moor or make contact with
      another conveyance while outside Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aircraft, the conveyance did
      not land while outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written report

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If goods are required by the regulations to be
  reported under subsection (1) in writing, they shall be reported in
  the prescribed form with the prescribed information or in such form
  and with such information as is satisfactory to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain goods not subject to seizure

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Goods described in tariff item No. 9813.00.00 or
  9814.00.00 in the List of Tariff Provisions set out in the schedule to
  the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that are in the actual possession of a person
    arriving in Canada, or that form part of his baggage, where the
    person and his baggage are being carried on board the same
    conveyance,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that are not charged with duties, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the importation of which is not prohibited under
    the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or prohibited, controlled
    or regulated under any Act of Parliament other than this Act or the
    [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)

  may not be seized as forfeit under this Act by reason only that they
  were not reported under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 12, c. 41 (3rd Supp.), s. 119
- 1992, c. 28, s. 3
- 1996, c. 31, s. 75
- 1997, c. 36, s. 149
- 2001, c. 25, s. 12
- 2015, c. 3, s. 60(F)
- 2017, c. 11, s. 3
- [2022, c. 10, s. 305(E)](#1357507-1447462){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357507-1447462.html"}
:::
