[Previous
Version](section-12-20170619.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[12.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.01 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations for the purposes of sections 11 and 12,
including regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances under which persons,
  goods or classes thereof on board a conveyance, or classes thereof,
  are required to present themselves or to be reported, as the case may
  be, despite subsection 11(5) or 12(5); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining the expression "make contact with another
  conveyance" for the purposes of subsections 11(5) and 12(5) and
  prescribing the circumstances under which a conveyance or a class
  thereof makes contact with another conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 11, s. 4
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance information

- **[[12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before the arrival of a conveyance
  in Canada, the owner or person in charge of a conveyance who is
  prescribed or any other prescribed person shall give the Agency
  prescribed information about the conveyance and the persons and goods
  on board or expected to be on board the conveyance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is required to provide information under
  subsection (1) shall hold a valid carrier code unless they are exempt.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrier code --- requirements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An application for a carrier code shall be made in
  the prescribed form with the prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrier code --- issuance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue a carrier code to a person
  who applies for it if the application meets the requirements referred
  to in subsection (3) and the Minister is satisfied that the
  requirements and conditions prescribed under paragraph (8)(e) for the
  carrier code to be issued have been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Carrier code --- suspension,
  cancellation and reinstatement

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, subject to the regulations,
  suspend, cancel or reinstate a carrier code.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue a notification to any person
  who provides information under subsection (1) to require the person to
  take any specified measure with respect to the information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to comply

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The person to whom a notification is issued shall
  comply with the notification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations for the
  purposes of this section, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information that must be given under
    subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the persons or classes of persons who
    must give the information under subsection (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which the
    information must be given under subsection (1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time within which and the manner in
    which the information must be given under subsection (1);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} regarding the requirements and conditions that are
    to be met before a carrier code may be issued;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} regarding the persons or classes of persons who are
    exempt from holding a valid carrier code; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} regarding the manner and circumstances in which a
    carrier code may be suspended, cancelled or reinstated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 10, s. 6
- 2012, c. 31, s. 266
- [2022, c. 10, s. 306](#1357509-1447465){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357509-1447465.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12.1-20150506.html "Link to previous version of section 12.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to answer questions and
present goods

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** Every person who reports
goods under section 12 inside or outside Canada or is stopped by an
officer in accordance with section 99.1 shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} answer truthfully any question asked by an officer
  with respect to the goods; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an officer so requests, present the goods to the
  officer, remove any covering from the goods, unload any conveyance or
  open any part of the conveyance, or open or unpack any package or
  container that the officer wishes to examine.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 13
- 2001, c. 25, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on unloading before report

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall unload goods from a conveyance arriving in Canada until
  the goods have been reported in accordance with sections 12 and 13
  except where the safety of the conveyance, or the goods or persons on
  the conveyance, is threatened by collision, fire, the stress of
  weather or other similar circumstances or in such other circumstances
  as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of goods unloaded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a conveyance is unloaded in the circumstances
  described in subsection (1), the person in charge of the conveyance
  shall forthwith, in such manner as may be prescribed, report the
  conveyance, the goods that were so unloaded and any goods that remain
  on the conveyance at a customs office designated for that purpose.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of goods illegally imported

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** Any person who finds or
has in his possession goods that have been imported and who believes on
reasonable grounds that the provisions of this or any other Act of
Parliament that prohibits, controls or regulates the importation of
goods have not been complied with in respect of the goods or that duties
levied thereon have not been paid shall forthwith report to an officer
that he has found the goods or has them in his possession.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wreck deemed imported

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, any wreck that has come into Canada from
  outside Canada shall be deemed to have been imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of wreck and liability for
  duties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any wreck that has come into Canada from outside
  Canada is released to a person under section 61 of the [Wrecked,
  Abandoned or Hazardous Vessels Act](/eng/acts/W-12.3), that person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall forthwith report the delivery to an officer;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is, from the time of the delivery, liable for all
    duties thereon calculated at the rates applicable to the wreck at
    the time of the delivery.

- Definition of [[wreck]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[wreck]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} jetsam, flotsam, lagan and derelict found in, or on
    the shores of, the sea, any tidal water, or any of the inland
    waters;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cargo, stores and tackle of any vessel and of all
    parts of the vessel separated therefrom;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the property of shipwrecked persons; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any wrecked aircraft or any part thereof and cargo
    thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 16
- 2001, c. 26, s. 299
- [2019, c. 1, s. 140](#1114261-1195947){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1114261-1195947.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20070701.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Duties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139674 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods charged with duties from
importation

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Imported goods are charged with
  duties thereon from the time of importation thereof until such time as
  the duties are paid or the charge is otherwise removed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates of duties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, the rates of duties on imported
  goods shall be the rates applicable to the goods at the time they are
  accounted for under subsection 32(1), (2) or (5) or, where goods have
  been released in the circumstances set out in paragraph 32(2)(b), at
  the time of release.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Whenever the importer of the goods that have been
  released or any person authorized under paragraph 32(6)(a) or
  subsection 32(7) to account for goods becomes liable under this Act to
  pay duties on those goods, the owner of the goods at the time of
  release becomes jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable, with the
  importer or person authorized, to pay the duties.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 17
- 1992, c. 28, s. 4
- 2001, c. 25, s. 14
- 2004, c. 25, s. 120(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Liability for Duties on Goods Reported]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139684 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption of importation

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section, all goods reported under section 12
  shall be deemed to have been imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of person reporting goods
  short landed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and 20(2.1), any person
  who reports goods under section 12, and any person for whom that
  person acts as agent or employee while so reporting, are jointly and
  severally or solidarily liable for all duties levied on the goods
  unless one or the other of them proves, within the time that may be
  prescribed, that the duties have been paid or that the goods

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} were destroyed or lost prior to report or destroyed
    after report but prior to receipt in a place referred to in
    paragraph (c) or by a person referred to in paragraph (d);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} did not leave the place outside Canada from which
    they were to have been exported;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have been received in a customs office, sufferance
    warehouse, bonded warehouse or duty free shop;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have been received by a person who transports or
    causes to be transported within Canada goods in accordance with
    subsection 20(1);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} have been exported; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} have been released.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates of duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The rates of duties payable on goods under
  subsection (2) shall be the rates applicable to the goods at the time
  they were reported under section 12.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing the circumstances in which such bonds or other security as
  may be prescribed may be required from any person who is or may become
  liable for the payment of duties under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 18
- 2001, c. 25, s. 15
:::

### [Movement and Storage of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139702 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of goods before release

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 20, any person
  who is authorized by an officer or by any prescribed means to do so
  may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deliver goods that have been reported under section
    12 or cause them to be delivered from a customs office to another
    customs office or a sufferance warehouse;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} deliver such goods or cause them to be delivered
    from a sufferance warehouse to another sufferance warehouse;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where such goods are designated as ships' stores by
    regulations made under paragraph 99(g) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), remove them or cause them to be removed
    from a customs office or sufferance warehouse for use on board a
    conveyance of a class prescribed under that paragraph in accordance
    with regulations made under that paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} export such goods or cause them to be exported
    directly from a customs office or sufferance warehouse; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} where such goods are at a customs office, leave
    them at that office, subject to such storage charges as may be
    prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization to deliver goods

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In prescribed circumstances and under prescribed
  conditions, a person may be authorized by an officer or by any
  prescribed means to deliver goods or cause them to be delivered to the
  place of business of the importer, owner or consignee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Movement and storage of goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 20, where goods that have been
  reported under section 12 have been described in the prescribed form
  at a customs office designated for that purpose, any person who is
  authorized by an officer or by any prescribed means to do so may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deliver the goods or cause them to be delivered
    from a customs office or sufferance warehouse to a bonded warehouse
    or duty free shop;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} deliver them or cause them to be delivered from a
    bonded warehouse to another bonded warehouse or to a duty free shop
    or from a duty free shop to another duty free shop or to a bonded
    warehouse;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the goods are designated as ships' stores by
    regulations made under paragraph 99(g) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), remove them or cause them to be removed
    from a bonded warehouse for use on board a conveyance of a class
    prescribed under that paragraph in accordance with regulations made
    under that paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} export them or cause them to be exported directly
    from a duty free shop in accordance with regulations made under
    section 30; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} export them or cause them to be exported directly
    from a bonded warehouse.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties removed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Goods that are removed as ships' stores under
  paragraph (1)(c) or exported under paragraph (1)(d) are, from the time
  of their exportation, no longer charged with duties.

- [(4) and (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1995, c. 41, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 19
- 1993, c. 25, s. 69
- 1995, c. 41, s. 3
- 1997, c. 36, s. 150
- 2001, c. 25, s. 16
- [2022, c. 10, s. 308(F)](#1357516-1447473){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357516-1447473.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statistics

- **[[19.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, any person
  who is authorized by an officer under subsection 19(2) to deliver
  goods or cause them to be delivered to a bonded warehouse shall,
  before the delivery, furnish an officer at a customs office with the
  statistical code for the goods determined by reference to the Coding
  System established pursuant to section 22.1 of the [Statistics
  Act](/eng/acts/S-19).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The statistical code referred to in subsection (1)
  shall be furnished in the prescribed form and manner of filing with
  the prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  exempting persons or goods, or classes thereof, from the requirements
  of subsection (1) subject to such conditions, if any, as are specified
  in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 67
- [2022, c. 10, s. 309](#1357518-1447474){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357518-1447474.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 19.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transportation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139734 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation of goods

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  in such circumstances as may be prescribed, every person who
  transports or causes to be transported within Canada goods that have
  been imported but have not been released shall do so subject to such
  conditions and subject to such bonds or other security as may be
  prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of transporter

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2.1), every person who
  transports or causes to be transported within Canada goods, other than
  goods to which paragraph 32(2)(b) applies, that have been imported but
  have not been released is liable for all duties on the goods unless
  the person proves, within the time that may be prescribed, that the
  goods were

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} destroyed while being so transported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} received in a customs office, sufferance warehouse,
    bonded warehouse or duty free shop;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where the goods are designated as ships' stores by
    regulations made under paragraph 99(g) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), received on board a conveyance of a
    class prescribed under that paragraph for use on the conveyance in
    accordance with regulations made under that paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} received by another person who is entitled under
    subsection (1) to transport such goods; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If a person transports within Canada goods to which
  paragraph 32(2)(b) applies, which the person is required to report
  under section 12 but which have not been released, the person is
  liable for all duties on the goods unless the person proves, within
  the time that may be prescribed, that the goods were

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} destroyed while being transported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} received in a customs office, bonded warehouse or
    duty free shop;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the goods are designated as ships' stores by
    regulations made under paragraph 99(g) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), received on board a conveyance of a
    class prescribed under that paragraph for use on the conveyance in
    accordance with regulations made under that paragraph;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} exported; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} received at the place of business of the importer,
    owner or consignee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates of duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The rates of duties payable on goods under
  subsection (2) shall be the rates applicable to the goods at the time
  they were reported under section 12.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 20
- 1995, c. 41, s. 4
- 1997, c. 36, s. 151
- 2001, c. 25, s. 17
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officer's access to goods

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
transports or causes to be transported within Canada goods that have
been imported but have not been released shall, where an officer so
requests, afford the officer free access to any premises or place under
his control that is attached to or forms part of any place where such
goods are reported, loaded, unloaded or stored, and open any package or
container of such goods or remove any covering therefrom.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  following persons shall keep the prescribed records at their place of
  business in Canada or at any other place that the Minister may
  designate, for the prescribed period and in the prescribed manner, and
  shall on the request of an officer make them available to the officer,
  within the time specified by the officer, and answer truthfully any
  questions asked by the officer about those records:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who transports or causes to be transported
    goods into Canada; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who transports or causes to be transported
    within Canada goods that have been imported but have not been
    released.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, subject to such terms and
  conditions as he may specify, exempt any person or class of persons
  from the requirement to keep records or from the requirement to keep
  records in Canada where he deems it unnecessary or impracticable to
  keep records or to keep them in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 22
- 1995, c. 41, s. 5
- 2001, c. 25, s. 18
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transportation over territory outside
Canada

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** Goods that are
transported from one place in Canada to another place in Canada over
territory or waters outside Canada in accordance with such terms and
conditions and subject to such bonds or other security as may be
prescribed shall be treated, with respect to their liability to or
exemption from duties, as if they had been transported entirely within
Canada.

### [Warehouses and Duty Free Shops]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139769 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the
  Minister may, where he deems it necessary or desirable to do so, issue
  to any person qualified under the regulations a licence for the
  operation of any place

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a sufferance warehouse for the examination of
    imported goods that have not been released, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1995, c. 41, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} as a duty free shop for the sale of goods free of
    certain duties or taxes levied on goods under the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the
    [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Excise Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or any other law relating to customs, to
    persons who are about to leave Canada

  and may specify in the licence any restriction as to the classes of
  goods that may be received therein or the circumstances in which goods
  may be received therein.

- Definition of [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} not to apply

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The definition [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  subsection 2(1) does not apply for the purposes of paragraph (1)(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, subject to the regulations, amend,
  suspend, renew, cancel or reinstate a licence issued under subsection
  (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 24
- 1993, c. 25, s. 70
- 1995, c. 41, s. 6
- 2001, c. 16, s. 2
- 2002, c. 22, s. 330
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sufferance warehouse operator's
obligation

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, the operator of a sufferance warehouse shall not refuse to
receive any goods brought to the warehouse that qualify under the terms
of his licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Price of goods sold in duty free shop

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of a duty free shop shall ensure that the prices of goods
  offered for sale at the duty free shop reflect the extent to which the
  goods have not been subject to duties and taxes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (1), [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means duties or taxes levied under the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the
  [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or any other law relating to customs.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 26
- 1993, c. 25, s. 71
- 2002, c. 22, s. 331
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officer's access to goods

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** The operator of a
sufferance warehouse, bonded warehouse or duty free shop shall, where an
officer so requests, afford the officer free access to the warehouse or
duty free shop or any premises or place under his control that is
attached to or forms part of the warehouse or duty free shop and open
any package or container of goods therein or remove any covering
therefrom.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of operator

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of a sufferance warehouse, bonded warehouse or duty free shop
  is liable for all duties or taxes levied under the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the [Excise Act](/eng/acts/E-14), the
  [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15) or any other law relating to customs on goods
  that have been received in the warehouse or duty free shop unless the
  operator proves that the goods

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are still in the warehouse or duty free shop;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have been destroyed while in the warehouse or duty
    free shop;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have been removed from the warehouse or duty free
    shop pursuant to section 19;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have been taken as a sample or seized under the
    [Firearms Act](/eng/acts/F-11.6) or any other Act of Parliament; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} have been released by an officer.

- [(1.1) and (1.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2002, c. 22, s.
  332\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The rates of duties or taxes payable on goods under
  subsection (1) shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the goods have been received in a sufferance
    warehouse, be the rates applicable to such goods at the time they
    were reported under section 12; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the goods have been received in a bonded
    warehouse or duty free shop, be the rates applicable to such goods
    at the time they were received therein.

- Definition of [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} not to apply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The definition [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  subsection 2(1) does not apply for the purposes of subsections (1) and
  (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 28
- 1993, c. 25, s. 72
- 1995, c. 39, s. 168
- 2001, c. 25, s. 19
- 2002, c. 22, ss. 332, 408
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1995, c. 41,
s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing qualifications as to citizenship and
  residence or any other qualifications that must be met by the operator
  of a sufferance warehouse or duty free shop;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the terms and conditions on which
  licences for the operation of sufferance warehouses or duty free shops
  may be issued under section 24, including the security that may be
  required of operators of the warehouses or shops, the duration of the
  licences and the fees or the manner of determining fees, if any, to be
  paid for the licences;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances in which licences for
  the operation of sufferance warehouses or duty free shops may be
  issued, amended, suspended, renewed, cancelled or reinstated;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} establishing standards for the operation of and the
  maintenance of the facilities of sufferance warehouses or duty free
  shops;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of acknowledging receipt of
  goods in sufferance warehouses or duty free shops;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} establishing the circumstances in which and the
  extent to which goods may be manipulated, unpacked, packed, altered or
  combined with other goods while in sufferance warehouses or duty free
  shops;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing facilities, equipment and personnel that
  must be provided at sufferance warehouses or duty free shops;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances in which the operator
  of a sufferance warehouse may refuse goods that are brought to the
  warehouse for safe-keeping;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} regulating the transfer of ownership of goods in duty
  free shops;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, with respect to goods, or classes of
  goods, that are offered for sale in a duty free shop, minimum
  proportions, by reference to quantity, value or other like standard,
  that must be of domestic origin;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} prescribing restrictions as to the classes of goods
  that may be received in sufferance warehouses;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} prescribing circumstances in which goods shall not be
  received in sufferance warehouses;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} regulating the provision of information by the
  operator of a duty free shop; and

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} otherwise regulating the operation of sufferance
  warehouses or duty free shops.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 30
- 1993, c. 25, s. 73
- 1995, c. 41, s. 7
:::

### [Release]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139830 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 19, no
goods shall be removed from a customs office, sufferance warehouse,
bonded warehouse or duty free shop by any person other than an officer
in the performance of his or her duties under this or any other Act of
Parliament unless the goods have been released by an officer or by any
prescribed means.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 31
- 2001, c. 25, s. 20
:::

### [Accounting and Payment of Duties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139835 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounting and payment of duties

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4)
  and any regulations made under subsection (6), and to section 33, no
  goods shall be released until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they have been accounted for by the importer or
    owner of the goods in the prescribed manner and, if they are to be
    accounted for in writing, in the prescribed form with the prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all duties thereon have been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release prior to accounting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In prescribed circumstances and under prescribed
  conditions, goods may be released prior to the accounting required
  under subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the importer or owner of the goods makes an interim
    accounting in the prescribed manner and in the prescribed form with
    the prescribed information or in the form and with the information
    that is satisfactory to the Minister; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the goods have been authorized by an officer or by
    any prescribed means for delivery to, and have been received at, the
    place of business of the importer, owner or consignee of the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounting after release

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If goods are released under subsection (2), they
  shall be accounted for within the prescribed time and in the manner
  described in paragraph (1)(a) by, in the case of goods to which
  paragraph (2)(a) applies, the person who made the interim accounting
  under that paragraph in respect of the goods and, in the case of goods
  to which paragraph (2)(b) applies, by the importer or owner of the
  goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of goods

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In such circumstances, and under such conditions, as
  may be prescribed, goods imported by courier or as mail may be
  released prior to the accounting required under subsection (1) and
  prior to the payment of duties thereon.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounting and payment of duties

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where goods are released under subsection (4),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who is authorized under paragraph (6)(a)
    or subsection (7) to account for the goods shall, within the
    prescribed time, account for the goods in the manner described in
    paragraph (1)(a) and that person or the importer or owner of the
    goods shall, within the prescribed time, pay duties on the goods, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where there is no person authorized under paragraph
    (6)(a) or subsection (7) to account for the goods, the importer or
    owner of the goods shall, within the prescribed time, account for
    the goods in the manner described in paragraph (1)(a) and shall,
    within the prescribed time, pay duties on the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed accounting

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Except in prescribed circumstances, where the
  importer or owner of mail that has been released as mail under
  subsection (4) takes delivery of the mail, the mail shall be deemed to
  have been accounted for under subsection (5) at the time of its
  release.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying persons or classes of persons who are
    authorized to account for goods under this section in lieu of the
    importer or owner thereof and prescribing the circumstances in which
    and the conditions under which such persons or classes of persons
    are so authorized; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances in which goods may be
    released without any requirement of accounting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization to account

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or an officer designated by the
  President for the purposes of this subsection may authorize any person
  not resident in Canada to account for goods under this section, in
  such circumstances and under such conditions as may be prescribed, in
  lieu of the importer or owner of those goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 32
- 1992, c. 28, s. 5
- 1995, c. 41, s. 8
- 2001, c. 25, s. 21
- 2005, c. 38, s. 63
- [2022, c. 10, s. 310](#1357520-1447480){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357520-1447480.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20051212.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statistics

- **[[32.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, every
  person who accounts for goods under subsection 32(1), (3) or (5)
  shall, at the time of accounting, furnish an officer at a customs
  office with the statistical code for the goods determined by reference
  to the Coding System established pursuant to section 22.1 of the
  [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The statistical code referred to in subsection (1)
  shall be furnished in the prescribed form and manner of filing with
  the prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  exempting persons or goods, or classes thereof, from the requirements
  of subsection (1) subject to such conditions, if any, as are specified
  in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 68
- [2022, c. 10, s. 311](#1357525-1447483){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357525-1447483.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 32.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction to declaration of origin

- **[[32.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  importer or owner of goods for which preferential tariff treatment
  under a free trade agreement has been claimed or any person authorized
  to account for those goods under paragraph 32(6)(a) or subsection
  32(7) shall, within ninety days after the importer, owner or person
  has reason to believe that a declaration of origin for those goods
  made under this Act is incorrect,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make a correction to the declaration of origin in
    the prescribed manner and in the prescribed form with the prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay any amount owing as duties as a result of the
    correction to the declaration of origin and any interest owing or
    that may become owing on that amount.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 14, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections to other declarations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations made under subsection (7), an
  importer or owner of goods or a person who is within a prescribed
  class of persons in relation to goods or is authorized under paragraph
  32(6)(a) or subsection 32(7) to account for goods shall, within ninety
  days after the importer, owner or person has reason to believe that
  the declaration of origin (other than a declaration of origin referred
  to in subsection (1)), declaration of tariff classification or
  declaration of value for duty made under this Act for any of those
  goods is incorrect,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make a correction to the declaration in the
    prescribed form and manner, with the prescribed information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay any amount owing as duties as a result of the
    correction to the declaration and any interest owing or that may
    become owing on that amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction treated as re-determination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A correction made under this section after the
  prescribed day is to be treated for the purposes of this Act as if it
  were a re-determination under paragraph 59(1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Four-year limit on correction
  obligation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The obligation under this section to make a
  correction in respect of imported goods ends four years after the
  goods are accounted for under subsection 32(1), (3) or (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction not to result in refund

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply to require or allow a
  correction that would result in a claim for a refund of duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Diversions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The obligation under this section to make a
  correction to a declaration of tariff classification includes an
  obligation to correct a declaration of tariff classification that is
  rendered incorrect by a failure, after the goods are accounted for
  under subsection 32(1), (3) or (5) or, in the case of prescribed
  goods, after the goods are released without accounting, to comply with
  a condition imposed under a tariff item in the List of Tariff
  Provisions set out in the schedule to the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) or under any regulations made under that
  Act in respect of a tariff item in that List.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing the circumstances in which certain goods are exempt from
  the operation of subsection (6) and the classes of goods in respect of
  which, the length of time for which and the conditions under which the
  exemptions apply.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a declaration of tariff classification is rendered
  incorrect by a failure referred to in subsection (6), for the purposes
  of paragraph (2)(b), duties do not include duties or taxes levied
  under the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15) or the [Special Import Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/S-15).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 82
- 1996, c. 33, s. 29
- 1997, c. 14, s. 36, c. 36, s. 152
- 2001, c. 25, s. 22
- 2002, c. 22, s. 333
- [2021, c. 23, s. 209](#1307437-1492790){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307437-1492790.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 312](#1357527-1447486){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357527-1447486.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32.2-20240219.html "Link to previous version of section 32.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Diversion of goods used as ships' stores

**[[32.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32.3 .sectionLabel}** If goods are removed
or caused to be removed for use as ships' stores under paragraph
19(1)(c) or (2)(c), and the goods are subsequently diverted to another
use, the person who diverted the goods shall, at the time of the
diversion,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} report the diversion to an officer at a customs
  office;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} account for the goods in the prescribed manner and in
  the prescribed form with the prescribed information; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay as duties on the goods an amount equal to the
  amount of duties that would be payable on like goods imported in like
  condition at the time of the diversion.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 25, s. 23
- [2022, c. 10, s. 313](#1357532-1447488){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357532-1447488.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32.3-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 32.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release prior to payment of duties

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  prescribed circumstances, goods may be released prior to the payment
  of duties levied on them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of duties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If goods are released under this section, the person
  who accounted for the goods under subsection 32(2) or (3) shall pay
  the duties levied on them within the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[duties]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  does not include the duties levied under

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 21.1(1) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), if they are paid and collected in
    accordance with subsection 21.1(2) of that Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsections 21.2(1) and (2) of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), if they are paid and collected in
    accordance with subsection 21.2(3) of that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 33
- 1992, c. 28, s. 6
- 2002, c. 22, s. 334
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[33.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c.
25, s. 24\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring accounting

**[[33.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.2 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or any
officer designated by the President for the purposes of this section
may, by notice served personally or by registered or certified mail,
require any person to account, within such reasonable time as may be
stipulated in the notice, in the manner described in paragraph 32(1)(a),
for any goods as may be designated in the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 7
- 2005, c. 38, s. 64
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33.2-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 33.2"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[33.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c.
25, s. 25\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest

- **[[33.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, any
  person who is liable to pay an amount of duties in respect of imported
  goods shall pay, in addition to the amount, interest at the specified
  rate, calculated on the outstanding balance of the amount,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods released prior to the
    accounting required under subsection 32(1), for the period beginning
    on the day after the prescribed day and ending on the day on which
    the amount has been paid in full; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other goods, for the period
    beginning on the day after the day on which the person became liable
    to pay the amount and ending on the day on which the amount has been
    paid in full.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- prescribed day

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The prescribed day referred to in paragraph (1)(a)
  falls

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the earliest, on the 12th day after the end of
    the period that begins on the 18th day of a month and ends on the
    17th day of the following month and that includes the earlier of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the goods in question are
      accounted for, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the last day on which those goods are required
      to be accounted for; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the latest, on the 18th day after the end of the
    period referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- interest-free period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, on the recommendation of
  the Minister of Finance, make regulations respecting the circumstances
  and conditions under which any person who is liable to pay an amount
  of duties in respect of imported goods is not liable to pay interest
  on that amount for the period specified in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 7
- 1994, c. 47, s. 70
- 1995, c. 41, s. 9
- 1997, c. 36, s. 154
- 2001, c. 25, s. 26
- [2021, c. 23, s. 210](#1307439-1492804){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307439-1492804.html"}
:::

Version](section-33.4-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 33.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring payment

**[[33.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.5 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or any
officer designated by the President for the purposes of this section
may, by notice served personally or by registered or certified mail,
require any person to pay any amount owing as duties, within such
reasonable time as may be stipulated in the notice, on any goods as may
be designated in the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 7
- 2005, c. 38, s. 65
:::

Version](section-33.5-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 33.5"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[33.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.6 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c.
25, s. 27\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

- **[[33.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or any officer
  designated by the President for the purposes of this section may at
  any time extend in writing the time prescribed by the regulations made
  under this Part for the accounting of goods or the payment of any
  amount owing as duties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of extension for accounting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the time within which a person must account for
  goods is extended under subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods shall be accounted for within the time as
    so extended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person accounts for the goods within the
    extended time, no penalty shall be imposed under section 109.1; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person fails to account for the goods within
    the time as so extended, the extension shall be deemed not to have
    been made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of extension for payment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the time within which a person must pay any
  amount owing as duties is extended under subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that amount shall be paid within the time as so
    extended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person pays that amount within the time as
    so extended, subsection 33.4(1) shall apply in respect of that
    amount as if the time had not been so extended, but interest payable
    under that subsection in respect of that amount shall be computed at
    the prescribed rate rather than at the specified rate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person fails to pay the amount within the
    time as so extended, the extension shall be deemed not to have been
    made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 28, s. 7
- 2001, c. 25, s. 28
- 2005, c. 38, s. 66
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33.7-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 33.7"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[33.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33.8 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c.
25, s. 29\]]{.Repealed}

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1995, c. 41,
s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security and conditions of release

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  in such circumstances as may be prescribed, no goods shall be released
  under subsection 32(2) or (4) or section 33 until such deposits, bonds
  or other security as may be prescribed is given, and any goods that
  are released under those provisions shall be released subject to such
  terms and conditions as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to satisfy terms and
  conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a deposit, bond or other security given under
  subsection (1) is subject to terms and conditions prescribed by
  regulations made under paragraph 166(1)(b), the person who has given
  the deposit, bond or other security shall satisfy those terms and
  conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 35
- 1995, c. 41, s. 11
- [2021, c. 23, s. 211](#1307441-1492810){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307441-1492810.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-35-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Marking of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139955 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to comply with marking
regulations

**[[35.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35.01 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
import goods that are required to be marked by any regulations made
under section 19 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) unless the
goods are marked in accordance with those regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 83
- 1997, c. 36, s. 155
:::

- **[[35.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 25, s.
  30\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring marking or compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or any officer designated by the
  President for the purposes of this section may, by notice served
  personally or by registered or certified mail, require any person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to mark imported goods in accordance with the
    regulations made under section 19 of the [Customs
    Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), within such reasonable time as may be
    stipulated in the notice, if there has been a failure to comply with
    section 35.01 with respect to the goods; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to comply with section 35.01 in respect of any
    goods designated in the notice that will subsequently be imported by
    the person.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 25, s. 30\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods imported from a NAFTA country

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a person imports goods of a prescribed class from
  a NAFTA country, that person is not liable to a penalty under section
  109.1 unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has previously failed to comply with
    section 35.01 in respect of imported goods and has been given notice
    pursuant to subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the goods with respect to which there has been a
    failure to comply with section 35.01 or a notice given pursuant to
    subsection (2) have been released without being marked in the manner
    referred to in section 35.01; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the imported goods have been marked in a deceptive
    manner so as to mislead another person as to the country or
    geographic area of origin of the goods.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 25, s. 30\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 83
- 1997, c. 36, s. 156
- 2001, c. 25, s. 30
- 2005, c. 38, s. 67
- [2022, c. 10, s. 314(E)](#1357534-1447490){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357534-1447490.html"}
:::

### [Origin of Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139975 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of origin

- **[[35.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made
  under subsection (4), proof of origin, in the prescribed form with the
  prescribed information and with the information, statements or proof
  required by any regulations made under subsection (4), shall be
  furnished in respect of all goods that are imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When furnished

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Proof of origin of goods shall be furnished under
  subsection (1) to an officer at such time and place and in such manner
  as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Who furnishes

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made under subsection (4),
  proof of origin of goods shall be furnished in accordance with
  subsection (1) by the importer or owner of the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of origin completed by
  importer

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} If an importer of goods for which preferential
  tariff treatment under the CPTPP or CUSMA will be claimed is the
  person who certifies that the goods meet the rules of origin set out
  in, or contemplated by, the CPTPP or CUSMA, the importer shall do so
  in writing, in the prescribed form with the prescribed information,
  and on the basis of supporting documents that the importer has or
  supporting documents that are provided by the exporter or producer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council, on the recommendation of the
  Minister, may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying persons or classes of persons who are
    authorized to furnish proof of origin of goods under subsection (1)
    in lieu of the importer or owner thereof and prescribing the
    circumstances in which and the conditions, if any, under which such
    persons or classes of persons are so authorized;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying, for the purpose of subsection (1), the
    information, statements or proof required in addition to the
    prescribed information; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exempting persons or goods, or classes thereof,
    from the requirements of subsection (1) subject to such conditions,
    if any, as are specified in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Denial or withdrawal of preferential
  tariff treatment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Preferential tariff treatment under a free trade
  agreement may be denied or withdrawn in respect of goods for which
  that treatment is claimed if the importer, owner or other person
  required to furnish proof of origin of the goods under this section
  fails to comply with any provision of this Act or the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), or any regulation made under either of
  those Acts, concerning that preferential tariff treatment.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 14, s. 37\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1988, c. 65, s. 69
- 1992, c. 28, s. 9
- 1993, c. 44, s. 84
- 1996, c. 33, s. 30
- 1997, c. 14, s. 37

- 2018, c. 23, s. 21

:::

### [Abandoned Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-139995 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Abandonment of goods to the Crown

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner of goods that have been imported but have not been released may,
  with the authorization of an officer and subject to the conditions set
  out in subsection (2), abandon the goods to Her Majesty in right of
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of abandonment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who abandons goods to Her Majesty under
  subsection (1) is liable for all reasonable expenses incurred by Her
  Majesty in the disposal of the goods where they are disposed of
  otherwise than by sale.

### [Unclaimed Goods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140001 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unclaimed goods

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Goods,
  other than goods of a prescribed class, that have not been removed
  from a customs office, sufferance warehouse or duty free shop within
  such period of time as may be prescribed may be deposited by an
  officer in a place of safe-keeping designated by the Minister for that
  purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unclaimed goods in a bonded warehouse

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods, other than goods of a class prescribed by
  regulations made under subparagraph 99(f)(xii) of the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), that have not been removed from a bonded
  warehouse within such time as may be prescribed by regulations made
  under subparagraph 99(f)(xi) of that Act may be deposited by an
  officer in a place of safe-keeping designated by the Minister for that
  purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of prescribed period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may extend any period of time prescribed
  pursuant to subsection (1) or (2) in respect of any particular goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deeming provision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A place of safe-keeping referred to in this section
  shall, for the purposes of this Act, be deemed to be a customs office.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 37
- 1993, c. 25, s. 74
- 1995, c. 41, s. 12
- 1997, c. 36, s. 157
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk and storage charges

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Goods
  that are deposited in a place of safe-keeping under section 37 shall
  be kept there at the risk of the owner and importer of those goods,
  and the owner and importer are jointly and severally, or solidarily,
  liable for any storage charges that may be prescribed and any expenses
  incurred in moving the goods from the customs office, sufferance
  warehouse, bonded warehouse or duty free shop to the place of
  safe-keeping.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No removal until expenses paid

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No goods shall be removed by any person other than an
  officer from a place of safe-keeping referred to in section 37 until
  the charges and expenses referred to in subsection (1) have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 38
- 2004, c. 25, s. 121(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unclaimed goods forfeit

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Goods
  that have not been removed from a place of safe-keeping referred to in
  section 37 within such period of time after they were deposited
  therein as may be prescribed are, at the termination of that period of
  time, forfeit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenses of disposal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The importer of goods that are forfeit under
  subsection (1) and the owner of those goods at the time of forfeiture
  are jointly and severally, or solidarily, liable for all reasonable
  expenses incurred by Her Majesty in right of Canada in the disposal of
  the goods if they are disposed of otherwise than by sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 39
- 2004, c. 25, s. 122(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-39-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 39"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Goods of a Prescribed Class]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140027 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods forfeit if not removed

- **[[39.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Goods of a prescribed class that
  have not been removed from a customs office, sufferance warehouse or
  duty free shop within such period of time as may be prescribed are, at
  the end of that period of time, forfeit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods in bonded warehouse forfeit if
  not removed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods of a class prescribed by regulations made under
  subparagraph 99(f)(xii) of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011)
  that have not been removed from a bonded warehouse within such period
  of time as may be prescribed by regulations made under subparagraph
  99(f)(xi) of that Act are, at the end of that period of time, forfeit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 25, s. 75
- 1995, c. 41, s. 13
- 1997, c. 36, s. 158
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140035 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importers' records

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who imports goods or causes goods to be imported for sale or
  for any industrial, occupational, commercial, institutional or other
  like use or any other use that may be prescribed shall keep at the
  person's place of business in Canada or at any other place that may be
  designated by the Minister any records in respect of those goods in
  any manner and for any period of time that may be prescribed and
  shall, where an officer so requests, make them available to the
  officer, within the time specified by the officer, and answer
  truthfully any questions asked by the officer in respect of the
  records.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's request

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Minister, a person has not
  kept records in accordance with subsection (1), the Minister may
  request that person to comply with that subsection in respect of the
  records.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to keep records

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following persons shall keep at their place of
  business or at any other place that may be designated by the Minister
  the prescribed records with respect to the prescribed goods, in the
  manner and for the period that may be prescribed, and shall, where an
  officer requests, make them available to the officer, within the time
  specified by the officer, and answer truthfully any questions asked by
  the officer in respect of the records:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is granted a licence under section 24;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who receives goods authorized for delivery
    to the person's place of business in the circumstances set out in
    paragraph 32(2)(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person who is authorized under paragraph 32(6)(a)
    or subsection 32(7) to account for goods;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person who is granted a certificate under section
    90 of the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a person who is granted a licence under section 91
    of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's request

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the opinion of the Minister, a person has
  not kept records in respect of goods in accordance with subsection
  (3), the Minister may request that person to comply with that
  subsection in respect of the goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 40
- 1992, c. 28, s. 10
- 1993, c. 44, s. 85
- 1995, c. 41, s. 15
- 1997, c. 36, s. 159
- 2001, c. 25, s. 31
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention of goods

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  goods imported by or on behalf of a person to whom a request is made
  under subsection 40(2) at any time after the request is made may be
  detained by an officer at the expense of that person until the request
  is complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of detained goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Goods that are detained under subsection (1) may be
  deposited in a place of safe-keeping in accordance with subsection
  37(1) as if they were unclaimed and may be dealt with thereafter under
  sections 37 to 39.

Definition of [[dwelling-house]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[dwelling-house]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the whole
  or any part of a building or structure that is kept or occupied as a
  permanent or temporary residence, and includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a building within the curtilage of a dwelling-house
    that is connected to it by a doorway or by a covered and enclosed
    passageway; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a unit that is designed to be mobile and to be used
    as a permanent or temporary residence and that is being used as such
    a residence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspections

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An officer, or an officer within a class of officers,
  designated by the President for the purposes of this section, may at
  all reasonable times, for any purpose related to the administration or
  enforcement of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inspect, audit or examine any record of a person
    that relates or may relate to the information that is or should be
    in the records of the person or to any amount paid or payable under
    this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} examine property in an inventory of a person and
    any property or process of, or matter relating to, the person, an
    examination of which may assist the officer in determining the
    accuracy of the inventory of the person or in ascertaining the
    information that is or should be in the records of the person or any
    amount paid or payable by the person under this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (3), enter any premises or
    place where any business is carried on, any property is kept,
    anything is done in connection with any business or any records are
    or should be kept; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} require the owner or manager of the property or
    business and any other person on the premises or place to give the
    officer all reasonable assistance and to answer truthfully any
    question, and, for that purpose, require the owner, manager or other
    person designated by the owner or manager to attend at the premises
    or place with the officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If any premises or place referred to in paragraph
  (2)(c) is a dwelling-house, an officer may not enter that
  dwelling-house without the consent of the occupant except under the
  authority of a warrant under subsection (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application by
  the Minister, a judge may issue a warrant authorizing an officer to
  enter a dwelling-house subject to the conditions that may be specified
  in the warrant, if the judge is satisfied by information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are reasonable grounds to believe that the
    dwelling-house is a premises or place referred to in paragraph
    (2)(c);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house is necessary; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house has been, or there
    are reasonable grounds to believe that entry into the dwelling-house
    will be, refused.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other access to document

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the judge is not satisfied that entry into that
  dwelling-house is necessary for any purpose relating to the
  administration or enforcement of this Act but is satisfied that access
  to a document or property that is or should be kept in the
  dwelling-house has been or may be expected to be refused, the judge
  may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} order the occupant of the dwelling-house to provide
    the officer with reasonable access to any document or property; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make any other order that is appropriate in the
    circumstances to carry out the purposes of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 42
- 2001, c. 25, s. 32
- 2005, c. 38, s. 68
:::

:::

### [Verifications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140081 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methods of verification

**[[42.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.01 .sectionLabel}** An officer, or an
officer within a class of officers, designated by the President for the
purposes of this section may conduct a verification of origin (other
than a verification of origin referred to in section 42.1), verification
of tariff classification or verification of value for duty in respect of
imported goods in the manner that is prescribed and may for that purpose
at all reasonable times enter any prescribed premises.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 36, s. 160
- 2001, c. 25, s. 33
- 2005, c. 38, s. 69
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42.01-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 42.01"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Verifications under a Free Trade Agreement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140086 .Subheading}

#### [Origin of Goods, Duty Relief and Drawback]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140087 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methods of verification

- **[[42.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any officer, or any officer within a
  class of officers, designated by the President for the purposes of
  this section, or any person, or any person within a class of persons,
  designated by the President to act on behalf of such an officer, may,
  subject to the prescribed conditions,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under a free trade agreement, other
    than a free trade agreement referred to in subsection (1.1), is
    claimed

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} by entering any prescribed premises or place at
      any reasonable time, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the prescribed manner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} enter any prescribed premises or place at any
    reasonable time to verify the amount, if any, of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a relief under section 89 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) from the payment of any duties payable
      in respect of imported goods that are subsequently exported to a
      NAFTA country, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a drawback under section 113 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) of duties paid in respect of imported
      goods that are subsequently exported to a NAFTA country; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} enter any prescribed premises or place at any
    reasonable time to verify the amount, if any, of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a relief under section 89 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) from the payment of any duties payable
      in respect of imported goods that are subsequently exported to a
      CUSMA country, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a drawback under section 113 of the [Customs
      Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) of duties paid in respect of imported
      goods that are subsequently exported to a CUSMA country.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Method of verification --- certain
  agreements

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Any officer, or any officer within a class of
  officers, designated by the President for the purposes of this
  section, or any person, or any person within a class of persons,
  designated by the President to act on behalf of such an officer, may,
  subject to any prescribed conditions, conduct any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under CEFTA is claimed, by requesting
    in writing that the customs administration of the EFTA state of
    export conduct a verification and provide an opinion as to whether
    the goods are originating within the meaning of Annex C of CEFTA;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under CUFTA 2017 is claimed, by
    requesting in writing that the customs administration of Ukraine
    conduct a verification and provide a written report as to whether
    the goods are originating within the meaning of Chapter 3 of CUFTA
    2017;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under CUFTA is claimed, by requesting
    in writing that the customs administration of Ukraine conduct a
    verification and provide a written report as to whether the goods
    are originating within the meaning of Chapter 3 of CUFTA;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under CETA is claimed, by requesting
    in writing that the customs administration of the EU country or
    other CETA beneficiary of export conduct a verification and provide
    a written report as to whether the goods are originating within the
    meaning of the Protocol on Rules of Origin and Origin Procedures of
    CETA; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a verification of origin of goods for which
    preferential tariff treatment under CUKTCA is claimed, by requesting
    in writing that the customs administration of the CUKTCA beneficiary
    of export conduct a verification and provide a written report as to
    whether the goods are originating within the meaning of the Protocol
    on Rules of Origin and Origin Procedures of CETA as incorporated by
    reference in CUKTCA.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of preferential tariff
  treatment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an exporter, producer or prescribed importer of
  goods that are subject to a verification of origin under paragraph
  (1)(a) fails to comply with the prescribed requirements or, in the
  case of a verification of origin under subparagraph (1)(a)(i), does
  not consent to the verification of origin in the prescribed manner and
  within the prescribed time, preferential tariff treatment under a free
  trade agreement, other than a free trade agreement referred to in
  subsection (1.1), may be denied or withdrawn from the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of preferential tariff
  treatment under CEFTA

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Preferential tariff treatment under a free trade
  agreement referred to in subsection (1.1) may be denied or withdrawn
  from the goods in any of the following circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CEFTA, if the EFTA state of export
    fails to conduct a verification or provide an opinion as to whether
    the goods are originating;

  - [(a.01)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CUFTA 2017, if Ukraine fails to
    conduct a verification or provide a written report as to whether the
    goods are originating;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CUFTA, if Ukraine fails to conduct
    a verification or provide a written report as to whether the goods
    are originating;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CETA, if the EU country or other
    CETA beneficiary of export fails to conduct a verification or
    provide a written report as to whether the goods are originating;

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} in the case of CUKTCA, if the CUKTCA beneficiary
    of export fails to conduct a verification or provide a written
    report as to whether the goods are originating;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an officer or other person designated under
    subsection (1.1) is unable to determine whether the goods are
    originating; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 86
- 1995, c. 41, s. 16
- 1997, c. 14, s. 38, c. 36, s. 161
- 2005, c. 38, s. 70
- 2009, c. 6, s. 24
- 2017, c. 6, s. 83, c. 8, ss. 21, 43
- 2018, c. 23, s. 22

:::
