#### [Statement of Origin]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140113 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of origin

- **[[42.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  completion of a verification of origin under paragraph 42.1(1)(a), an
  officer designated under subsection 42.1(1) shall provide the
  exporter, producer or prescribed importer whose goods are subject to
  the verification of origin with a statement as to whether the goods
  are eligible, under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), for the
  preferential tariff treatment that was claimed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Basis of statement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A statement referred to in subsection (1) must
  include any findings of fact or law on which it was based.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 86
- 1997, c. 14, s. 38, c. 36, s. 162
- 2012, c. 18, s. 26(F)
- 2018, c. 23, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42.2-20121001.html "Link to previous version of section 42.2"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Effective Date of Re-determination of Origin]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140121 .Subheading}

Definition of [[customs administration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[42.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[customs administration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
  meaning assigned to that expression by Article 514 of NAFTA, Article
  E-14 of CCFTA, Article V.14 of CCRFTA or Article 5.1 of CHFTA, as the
  case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of redetermination or
  further redetermination of origin of goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a redetermination or
  further redetermination of origin does not take effect until notice of
  it is given to the importer of the goods and any person who completed
  and signed a Certificate of Origin for the goods if the result of the
  redetermination or further redetermination of origin made under
  subsection 59(1) in respect of goods for which preferential tariff
  treatment under NAFTA, CCFTA, CCRFTA or CHFTA is claimed and that are
  the subject of a verification of origin under this Act is that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the goods are not eligible for that preferential
    tariff treatment on the basis of the tariff classification or value
    of one or more materials used in their production; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that tariff classification or value differs from
    the tariff classification or value applied to those materials by the
    NAFTA country from which the goods were exported, from Chile, from
    Costa Rica or from Honduras, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A redetermination or further redetermination of
  origin referred to in subsection (2) shall not be applied to goods
  imported before the date on which the notice was given if the customs
  administration of the NAFTA country from which the goods were
  exported, of Chile, of Costa Rica or of Honduras, as the case may be,
  has, before that date,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} given an advance ruling under Article 509 of NAFTA,
    Article E-09 of CCFTA, Article V.9 of CCRFTA, paragraph 1 of Article
    5.10 or paragraph 11 of Article 6.2 of CHFTA as the case may be, or
    given another ruling referred to in paragraph 12 of Article 506 of
    NAFTA, paragraph 12 of Article E-06 of CCFTA, paragraph 15 of
    Article V.6 of CCRFTA, or paragraph 15 of Article 5.7 of CHFTA, as
    the case may be, on the tariff classification or value of the
    materials referred to in subsection (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} given consistent treatment with respect to the
    tariff classification or value of the materials referred to in
    subsection (2) on their importation into the NAFTA country, Chile,
    Costa Rica or Honduras, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Postponement of effective date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The date on which a redetermination or further
  redetermination of origin referred to in subsection (2) takes effect
  shall be postponed for a period not exceeding ninety days if the
  importer of the goods that are the subject of the redetermination or
  further redetermination or any person who completed and signed a
  Certificate of Origin for the goods establishes to the satisfaction of
  the Minister that the importer or the person, as the case may be, has
  relied in good faith, to the detriment of the importer or person, on
  the tariff classification or value applied to the materials referred
  to in that subsection by the customs administration of the NAFTA
  country from which the goods were exported, of Chile, Costa Rica or of
  Honduras, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of detrimental reliance under
  CEFTA

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an exporter of goods from an EFTA state
  demonstrates, to the satisfaction of the Minister, that it has relied,
  in good faith and to its detriment, on a ruling made by the Agency or
  the customs administration of an EFTA state with respect to the tariff
  classification or value of a non-originating material used in the
  production of goods, a redetermination of origin by the Agency in
  respect of goods for which preferential tariff treatment under CEFTA
  is claimed shall apply only to importations of the goods made after
  the date of the redetermination.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 86
- 1997, c. 14, s. 38, c. 36, s. 163
- 2001, c. 25, s. 34(F), c. 28, s. 27
- 2009, c. 6, s. 25
- 2014, c. 14, s. 24
:::

Version](section-42.3-20090701.html "Link to previous version of section 42.3"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Denial or Withdrawal of Benefit of Preferential Tariff Treatment Under Certain Free Trade Agreements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140139 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[identical
goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[42.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[identical goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning
  assigned to that expression by the provision, set out in column 2 of
  Part 2 of the schedule, of an agreement set out in column 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Denial or withdrawal of benefit ---
  specified countries

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 24 of the [Customs
  Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011), the Minister may, subject to any
  prescribed conditions, deny or withdraw preferential tariff treatment
  under an agreement set out in column 1 of Part 2 of the schedule in
  respect of goods for which that treatment is claimed if the exporter,
  producer or prescribed importer of the goods has made false
  representations that identical goods exported, produced or imported by
  that exporter, producer or prescribed importer and for which that
  treatment was claimed were eligible for that treatment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 44, s. 86
- 1997, c. 14, s. 38, c. 36, s. 164
- 2001, c. 28, s. 27
- 2009, c. 16, s. 32
- 2010, c. 4, s. 26
- 2012, c. 18, s. 27
- 2018, c. 23, s. 24
:::

[Previous
Version](section-42.4-20121001.html "Link to previous version of section 42.4"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Duty Evasion]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1253209 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[duty
evasion]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[42.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.5 .sectionLabel}** In sections 42.6 and
42.7, [[duty evasion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the evasion of
anti-dumping, countervailing or safeguard duties that are imposed by a
CUSMA country other than Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [1996, c. 33, s. 32; 1997, c. 14, s. 38](#1238029-1253213){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238029-1253213.html"}
- [2020, c. 1, s. 120](#1238029-1253214){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238029-1253214.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty evasion verification

- **[[42.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  requested by a CUSMA country under paragraph 5 of Article 10.7 of
  CUSMA, any officer, or any officer within a class of officers,
  designated by the President for the purposes of this section may ---
  for the purpose of enabling the requesting CUSMA country to determine
  whether goods exported from Canada to that country are subject to
  anti-dumping, countervailing, or safeguard duties imposed by that
  country --- conduct a duty evasion verification that includes, among
  other things, obtaining information from an exporter or producer of
  goods in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 7(a) of Article 10.7 of CUSMA

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The duty evasion verification may be conducted only
  if Canada and the requesting CUSMA country have mutually agreed to
  conditions and procedures and the verification is conducted in
  accordance with those conditions and procedures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to enter

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In conducting the duty evasion verification, the
  officer may enter the premises of an exporter or producer of goods.

::: HistoricalNote
- [1996, c. 33, s. 32; 1997, c. 14, s. 38](#1238029-1253223){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238029-1253223.html"}
- [2020, c. 1, s. 120](#1238029-1253224){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238029-1253224.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

**[[42.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.7 .sectionLabel}** After a duty evasion
verification has been completed, an officer shall provide the requesting
CUSMA country with a report that contains any relevant information
obtained from an exporter or producer of goods in Canada during the
verification.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2020, c. 1, s. 120](#1238029-1253228){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1238029-1253228.html"}
:::

#### [Production of Documents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140148 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of records

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, for any purpose related to the administration or
  enforcement of this Act, including the collection of any amount owing
  under this Act by any person, by notice served personally or sent by
  registered or certified mail, require any person to provide any record
  at a place specified by the Minister and within any reasonable time
  that may be stipulated in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is required to provide any records,
  books, letters, accounts, invoices, statements or other documents or
  information under subsection (1) shall, notwithstanding any other law
  to the contrary but subject to subsection (3), do so as required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 232 of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The definitions [[lawyer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and
  [[solicitor-client privilege]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection
  232(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), and subsection 232(2)
  of that Act, apply with respect to a requirement under subsection (1)
  as if the reference in subsection 232(2) to section 231.2 of that Act
  were a reference to this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 43
- 2000, c. 30, s. 160
- 2001, c. 25, s. 35
:::

### [Advance Rulings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140158 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance rulings

- **[[43.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any officer, or any officer within a
  class of officers, designated by the President for the purposes of
  this section shall, before goods are imported, on application by any
  member of a prescribed class that is made within the prescribed time,
  in the prescribed form and manner of filing with the prescribed
  information, give an advance ruling with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the goods qualify as originating goods and
    are entitled to the benefit of preferential tariff treatment under a
    free trade agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods exported from a country or
    territory set out in column 1 of Part 3 of the schedule, any matter,
    other than those referred to in paragraphs (a) and (c), concerning
    those goods that is set out in the provision set out in column 2;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the tariff classification of the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting advance rulings, including regulations respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the application of an advance ruling;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the modification or revocation of an advance
    ruling, including whether the modification or revocation applies
    retroactively;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the authority to request supplementary information
    in respect of an application for an advance ruling; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which the issuance of advance
    rulings may be declined or postponed.

::: HistoricalNote

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Calculation of Duty]{.HTitleText1} {#h-140173 .Part}

### [Duties Based on Percentage Rates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-140174 .Subheading}

#### [Valuation for Duty]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140175 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Ad valorem]{.otherLang lang="la"} rates
of duty

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** If duties, other than
duties or taxes levied under the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the
[Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1) or the [Select Luxury Items Tax
Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35), are imposed on goods at a percentage rate, such
duties shall be calculated by applying the rate to a value determined in
accordance with sections 45 to 55.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 44
- 2002, c. 22, s. 335
- [2022, c. 10, s. 153](#1356787-1374190){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356787-1374190.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20030701.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140180 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section and sections 46 to 55,

  [[computed value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[computed value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of goods,
      the value of the goods determined in accordance with section
      52; ([valeur reconstituée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[country of export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[country of export]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
      goods, the country from which the goods are shipped directly to
      Canada; ([pays d'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deductive value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deductive value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of
      goods, the value of the goods determined in accordance with
      subsection 51(2); ([valeur de référence]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[goods of the same class or kind]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[goods of the same class or kind]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
      relation to goods being appraised, means imported goods that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are within a group or range of imported goods
        produced by a particular industry or industry sector that
        includes identical goods and similar goods in relation to the
        goods being appraised, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} section 51, were produced in any country and
          exported from any country, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} section 52, were produced in and exported
          from the same country as the country in and from which the
          goods being appraised were produced and
          exported; ([marchandises de même nature ou de même
          espèce]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[identical goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[identical goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to goods
      being appraised, means imported goods that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are the same in all respects, including
        physical characteristics, quality and reputation, as the goods
        being appraised, except for minor differences in appearance that
        do not affect the value of the goods,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} were produced in the same country as the
        country in which the goods being appraised were produced, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} were produced by or on behalf of the person by
        or on behalf of whom the goods being appraised were produced,

      but does not include imported goods where engineering, development
      work, art work, design work, plans or sketches undertaken in
      Canada were supplied, directly or indirectly, by the purchaser of
      those imported goods free of charge or at a reduced cost for use
      in connection with the production and sale for export of those
      imported goods; ([marchandises identiques]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[price paid or payable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[price paid or payable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of the
      sale of goods for export to Canada, means the aggregate of all
      payments made or to be made, directly or indirectly, in respect of
      the goods by the purchaser to or for the benefit of the
      vendor; ([prix payé ou à payer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes grow, manufacture and
      mine; ([produit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[purchaser in Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[purchaser in Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning
      assigned by the regulations; ([acheteur au
      Canada]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[similar goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[similar goods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to goods being
      appraised, means imported goods that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} closely resemble the goods being appraised in
        respect of their component materials and characteristics,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are capable of performing the same functions
        as, and of being commercially interchangeable with, the goods
        being appraised,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} were produced in the same country as the
        country in which the goods being appraised were produced, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} were produced by or on behalf of the person by
        or on behalf of whom the goods being appraised were produced,

      but does not include imported goods where engineering, development
      work, art work, design work, plans or sketches undertaken in
      Canada were supplied, directly or indirectly, by the purchaser of
      those imported goods free of charge or at a reduced cost for use
      in connection with the production and sale for export of those
      imported goods; ([marchandises semblables]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[sufficient information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sufficient information]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of the
      determination of any amount, difference or adjustment, means
      objective and quantifiable information that establishes the
      accuracy of the amount, difference or adjustment; ([renseignements
      suffisants]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transaction value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transaction value]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of goods,
      means the value of the goods determined in accordance with
      subsection 48(4). ([valeur transactionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods deemed to be identical goods or
  similar goods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and sections 46 to
  55, where there are no identical goods or similar goods, as the case
  may be, in relation to goods being appraised but there are goods that
  would be identical goods or similar goods, as the case may be, if they
  were produced by or on behalf of the person by or on behalf of whom
  the goods being appraised were produced, those goods shall be deemed
  to be identical goods or similar goods, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 46 to 55, persons are
  related to each other if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are individuals connected by blood
    relationship, marriage, common-law partnership or adoption within
    the meaning of subsection 251(6) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one is an officer or director of the other;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each such person is an officer or director of the
    same two corporations, associations, partnerships or other
    organizations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they are partners;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} one is the employer of the other;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} they directly or indirectly control or are
    controlled by the same person;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} one directly or indirectly controls or is
    controlled by the other;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any other person directly or indirectly owns, holds
    or controls five per cent or more of the outstanding voting stock or
    shares of each such person; or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} one directly or indirectly owns, holds or controls
    five per cent or more of the outstanding voting stock or shares of
    the other.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 45
- 1995, c. 41, s. 17
- 2000, c. 12, s. 96
:::

#### [Determination of Value for Duty]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140223 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of value for duty

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** The value for duty of
imported goods shall be determined in accordance with sections 47 to 55.

#### [Order of Consideration of Methods of Valuation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140226 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Primary basis of appraisal

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  value for duty of goods shall be appraised on the basis of the
  transaction value of the goods in accordance with the conditions set
  out in section 48.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary bases of appraisal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the value for duty of goods is not appraised in
  accordance with subsection (1), it shall be appraised on the basis of
  the first of the following values, considered in the order set out
  herein, that can be determined in respect of the goods and that can,
  under sections 49 to 52, be the basis on which the value for duty of
  the goods is appraised:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transaction value of identical goods that meets
    the requirements set out in section 49;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transaction value of similar goods that meets
    the requirements set out in section 50;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the deductive value of the goods; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the computed value of the goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request of importer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), on the written
  request of the importer of any goods being appraised made prior to the
  commencement of the appraisal of those goods, the order of
  consideration of the values referred to in paragraphs (2)(c) and (d)
  shall be reversed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Residual basis of appraisal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the value for duty of goods is not appraised on
  the basis of any of the values referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) to
  (d), the value for duty of those goods shall be appraised under
  section 53.

#### [Transaction Value of the Goods]{.HTitleText3} {#h-140240 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transaction value as primary basis of
appraisal

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6) and (7),
  the value for duty of goods is the transaction value of the goods if
  the goods are sold for export to Canada to a purchaser in Canada and
  the price paid or payable for the goods can be determined and if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are no restrictions respecting the
    disposition or use of the goods by the purchaser thereof, other than
    restrictions that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are imposed by law,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} limit the geographical area in which the goods
      may be resold, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} do not substantially affect the value of the
      goods;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sale of the goods by the vendor to the
    purchaser or the price paid or payable for the goods is not subject
    to some condition or consideration, with respect to the goods, in
    respect of which a value cannot be determined;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} when any part of the proceeds of any subsequent
    resale, disposal or use of the goods by the purchaser is to accrue,
    directly or indirectly, to the vendor, the price paid or payable for
    the goods includes the value of that part of the proceeds or the
    price is adjusted in accordance with paragraph (5)(a); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser and the vendor of the goods are not
    related to each other at the time the goods are sold for export or,
    where the purchaser and the vendor are related to each other at that
    time,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their relationship did not influence the price
      paid or payable for the goods, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the importer of the goods demonstrates that the
      transaction value of the goods meets the requirement set out in
      subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure in application of paragraph
  (1)(d)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the application of paragraph (1)(d), where the
  purchaser and the vendor of goods being appraised are related to each
  other at the time the goods are sold for export and the officer who is
  appraising the value for duty of the goods has grounds to believe that
  the requirement set out in subparagraph (1)(d)(i) is not met, the
  officer shall notify the importer of the goods of such grounds and, on
  the written request of the importer, the notification shall be in
  writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement for accepting transaction
  value where purchaser and vendor related

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(d)(ii), the
  transaction value of goods being appraised shall, taking into
  consideration any relevant factors including, without limiting the
  generality of the foregoing, such factors and differences as may be
  prescribed, closely approximate one of the following values that is in
  respect of identical goods or similar goods exported at the same or
  substantially the same time as the goods being appraised and is the
  value for duty of the goods to which it relates:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transaction value of identical goods or similar
    goods in a sale of those goods for export to Canada between a vendor
    and purchaser who are not related to each other at the time of the
    sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the deductive value of identical goods or similar
    goods; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the computed value of identical goods or similar
    goods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of transaction value

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The transaction value of goods shall be determined by
  ascertaining the price paid or payable for the goods when the goods
  are sold for export to Canada and adjusting the price paid or payable
  in accordance with subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment of price paid or payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The price paid or payable in the sale of goods for
  export to Canada shall be adjusted

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by adding thereto amounts, to the extent that each
    such amount is not already included in the price paid or payable for
    the goods, equal to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} commissions and brokerage in respect of the goods
      incurred by the purchaser thereof, other than fees paid or payable
      by the purchaser to his agent for the service of representing the
      purchaser abroad in respect of the sale,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the packing costs and charges incurred by the
      purchaser in respect of the goods, including the cost of cartons,
      cases and other containers and coverings that are treated for
      customs purposes as being part of the imported goods and all
      expenses of packing incident to placing the goods in the condition
      in which they are shipped to Canada,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the value of any of the following goods and
      services, determined in the manner prescribed, that are supplied,
      directly or indirectly, by the purchaser of the goods free of
      charge or at a reduced cost for use in connection with the
      production and sale for export of the imported goods, apportioned
      to the imported goods in a reasonable manner and in accordance
      with generally accepted accounting principles:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} materials, components, parts and other goods
        incorporated in the imported goods,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} tools, dies, moulds and other goods utilized in
        the production of the imported goods,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} any materials consumed in the production of the
        imported goods, and

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} engineering, development work, art work, design
        work, plans and sketches undertaken elsewhere than in Canada and
        necessary for the production of the imported goods,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} royalties and licence fees, including payments
      for patents, trademarks and copyrights, in respect of the goods
      that the purchaser of the goods must pay, directly or indirectly,
      as a condition of the sale of the goods for export to Canada,
      exclusive of charges for the right to reproduce the goods in
      Canada,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the value of any part of the proceeds of any
      subsequent resale, disposal or use of the goods by the purchaser
      thereof that accrues or is to accrue, directly or indirectly, to
      the vendor, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the cost of transportation of, the loading,
      unloading and handling charges and other charges and expenses
      associated with the transportation of, and the cost of insurance
      relating to the transportation of, the goods to the place within
      the country of export from which the goods are shipped directly to
      Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by deducting therefrom amounts, to the extent that
    each such amount is included in the price paid or payable for the
    goods, equal to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cost of transportation of, the loading,
      unloading and handling charges and other charges and expenses
      associated with the transportation of, and the cost of insurance
      relating to the transportation of, the goods from the place within
      the country of export from which the goods are shipped directly to
      Canada, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any of the following costs, charges or expenses
      if the cost, charge or expense is identified separately from the
      price paid or payable for the goods:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} any reasonable cost, charge or expense that is
        incurred for the construction, erection, assembly or maintenance
        of, or technical assistance provided in respect of, the goods
        after the goods are imported, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any duties and taxes paid or payable by reason
        of the importation of the goods or sale of the goods in Canada,
        including, without limiting the generality of the foregoing, any
        duties or taxes levied on the goods under the [Excise Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Special Import Measures
        Act](/eng/acts/S-15), the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011),
        the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Select Luxury
        Items Tax Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35) or any other law relating to
        customs; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by disregarding any rebate of, or other decrease
    in, the price paid or payable for the goods that is effected after
    the goods are imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of absence of sufficient
  information

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where there is not sufficient information to
  determine any of the amounts required to be added to the price paid or
  payable in respect of any goods being appraised, the value for duty of
  the goods shall not be appraised under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where information inaccurate

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Where an officer who is appraising the value for duty
  of goods believes on reasonable grounds that the information submitted
  in support of the transaction value of the goods as determined under
  subsection (4) is inaccurate, the officer shall determine, in
  accordance with the prescribed procedure, that the value for duty of
  the goods shall not be appraised under this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (2nd Supp.), s. 48
- 1994, c. 47, s. 71
- 1995, c. 41, s. 18
- 2002, c. 22, s. 336
- 2009, c. 10, s. 7
- [2014, c. 20, s. 366(E)](#1034903-1145458){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1034903-1145458.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 154](#1356791-1374192){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356791-1374192.html"}
:::
